## AnimatePalettes
if (resourceHeader == null) return;
byte* mainMem = GetMainMemPtr();
if (mainMem == null) return;
uint** list = *(uint***)(resourceHeader + 0x64);
if (list == null) return;
while (true)
{
uint* descriptors = list[0];
if (descriptors == null) break;
uint bank = (uint)(nuint)list[1];
uint descriptor = *descriptors;
while (descriptor != 0)
{
descriptor += 0x1000000;
uint updated = descriptor;
if ((byte)(descriptor >> 0x18) == (byte)(descriptor >> 0x10))
{
updated = descriptor & 0xffffff;
ushort* clut = (ushort*)(mainMem
+ (descriptor & 0xff) * 0x20
+ ((bank & 0xff) + 0xe10) * 0x200);
int count = (byte)(updated >> 8);
ushort first = clut[0];
for (int i = 1; i < count; i++)
{
clut[i - 1] = clut[i];
}
if (count >= 1) clut[count - 1] = first;
}
*descriptors = updated;
descriptors++;
descriptor = *descriptors;
}
list += 2;
}

## AreaVerb_00442F60
byte* flags = Arena(0x005e9000);
byte* bank = Arena(0x005e9840);
if ((flags[1] & 0x10) != 0)
{
bank[0] &= 1;
bank[1] &= 0xf8;
}
else
{

for (int bit = 0; bit < 7; bit++)
{

## Compile
byte[] source = System.Text.Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(ShaderSource);
nint entryPtr = Marshal.StringToHGlobalAnsi(entryPoint);
nint targetPtr = Marshal.StringToHGlobalAnsi(target);
nint namePtr = Marshal.StringToHGlobalAnsi("OpenForesia.hlsl");
try
{
ID3D10Blob* code = null;
ID3D10Blob* errors = null;
int hr;
fixed (byte* pSource = source)
{
hr = s_Compiler!.Compile(pSource, (nuint)source.Length, (byte*)namePtr,
null, (ID3DInclude*)null, (byte*)entryPtr, (byte*)targetPtr,
0, 0, &code, &errors);
}
if (hr < 0)
{
string message = errors != null
? Marshal.PtrToStringAnsi((nint)errors->GetBufferPointer()) ?? "(sans détail)"
: "(sans détail)";
throw new InvalidOperationException(
$"Compilation du shader {entryPoint} échouée (0x{hr:X8}) : {message}");
}
return new ComPtr<ID3D10Blob>(code);
}
finally
{
Marshal.FreeHGlobal(entryPtr);
Marshal.FreeHGlobal(targetPtr);
Marshal.FreeHGlobal(namePtr);
}
## ComputeDerivedStats
int extra = *(ushort*)(record + 0x144);
int dword1 = *(int*)(record + 0x124);
int stat0 = *(ushort*)(record + 0x120);
int stat1 = *(ushort*)(record + 0x122);
int stat6 = *(ushort*)(record + 0x12c);
int dword2 = *(int*)(record + 0x128);
int stat3 = dword1 >> 0x10;
int stat4 = dword2 & 0xffff;
int stat5 = dword2 >> 0x10;
*(short*)(record + 0x290) = (short)((extra + stat0) >> 2);
*(short*)(record + 0x292) =
(short)((((stat4 >> 2) + stat3 + stat4) >> 2) + extra >> 2);
*(short*)(record + 0x294) = (short)(((stat0 + stat3) >> 1) + extra >> 2);
int halfExtra = extra >> 1;
*(short*)(record + 0x296) =
(short)((((dword1 >> 0x12) + stat3 + stat4) >> 1) + halfExtra >> 2);
*(short*)(record + 0x298) = (short)((extra + stat5) >> 2);
int stat2 = dword1 & 0xffff;
*(short*)(record + 0x29e) =
(short)((((stat2 >> 2) + stat2 + stat1) >> 1) + halfExtra >> 2);
*(short*)(record + 0x29a) =
(short)((((stat1 >> 2) + stat2 + stat1) >> 2) + extra >> 2);
*(short*)(record + 0x29c) = (short)(((stat5 + stat2) >> 1) + extra >> 2);
short* derived = (short*)(record + 0x290);
for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
{
if (derived[i] > 999) derived[i] = 999;
}
*(uint*)(record + 0x210) = 0;
*(uint*)(record + 0x214) = 0;
*(uint*)(record + 0x218) = 0;
*(uint*)(record + 0x21c) = 0;
int slot7 = (stat6 >> 5) + (*(short*)(record + 0x2a6) >> 3);
*(ushort*)(record + 0x28e) = (ushort)slot7;
if (slot7 > 0xf) *(ushort*)(record + 0x28e) = 0xf;
*(ushort*)(record + 0x154) = (ushort)(*(short*)(record + 0x2a2) + (stat1 >> 3));
*(short*)(record + 0x156) = (short)(*(short*)(record + 0x2aa) >> 2);
CopyDwords((uint*)(record + 0x290), (uint*)(record + 0x130), 4);
## ComputeItemValue
uint value = *(ushort*)(slot + 0x88);

{

value += (uint)(v * 2);
}

}

}
return value;

## DecodeMsAdpcm
int numCoef = srcFormat->wNumCoef & 0xff;
int channels = srcFormat->wfx.nChannels & 0xff;
int destBits = destFormat->wBitsPerSample & 0xff;
int samplesPerBlock = srcFormat->wSamplesPerBlock;
short* aCoef = (short*)((byte*)srcFormat + 0x16);
int decodedSamples = 0;
if (srcByteBudget == 0) return 0;
short* delta = stackalloc short[2];
short* sample1 = stackalloc short[2];
short* sample2 = stackalloc short[2];
short* coef1 = stackalloc short[2];
short* coef2 = stackalloc short[2];
uint consumed = 0;
uint blockHeaderBytes = (uint)(channels * 7);
byte* s = src;
byte* d = dest;
while (true)
{
consumed += blockHeaderBytes;
if (consumed > srcByteBudget) break;
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++)
{
byte predictorIndex = *s++;
if (predictorIndex >= numCoef)
{
decodedSamples = 0;
goto finished;
}
coef1[ch] = aCoef[predictorIndex * 2];
coef2[ch] = aCoef[predictorIndex * 2 + 1];
}
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++) { delta[ch] = *(short*)s; s += 2; }
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++) { sample1[ch] = *(short*)s; s += 2; }
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++) { sample2[ch] = *(short*)s; s += 2; }
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++) d = WriteDecodedSample(d, sample2[ch], destBits);
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++) d = WriteDecodedSample(d, sample1[ch], destBits);
decodedSamples += 2;
bool readNewByte = true;
int pendingLowNibble = 0;
for (int sampleIndex = 2; sampleIndex < samplesPerBlock; sampleIndex++)
{
for (int ch = 0; ch < channels; ch++)
{
int nibble;
if (readNewByte)
{
consumed++;
if (consumed > srcByteBudget) goto finished;
int packed = (sbyte)*s++;
nibble = (short)packed >> 4;
pendingLowNibble = (packed << 0x1c) >> 0x1c;
readNewByte = false;
}
else
{
nibble = pendingLowNibble;
readNewByte = true;
}
short oldDelta = delta[ch];
short newDelta = (short)(int)((uint)(MsAdpcmAdaptationTable[nibble & 0xf] * oldDelta) >> 8);
delta[ch] = newDelta < 0x10 ? (short)0x10 : newDelta;
int predicted = ((coef2[ch] * sample2[ch] + coef1[ch] * sample1[ch]) >> 8) + oldDelta * nibble;
if (predicted >= 0x8000) predicted = 0x7fff;
else if (predicted < -0x8000) predicted = -0x8000;
d = WriteDecodedSample(d, (short)predicted, destBits);
sample2[ch] = sample1[ch];
sample1[ch] = (short)predicted;
}
decodedSamples++;
}
if (consumed >= srcByteBudget) break;
}
finished:
return ((destBits >> 3) << (channels >> 1)) * decodedSamples;

while (!reload && !relocated && !stop)
{
uint a, b, fromIndex, toIndex;
switch (edge)
{
case 0:
a = p0; b = p2; fromIndex = i0; toIndex = i2; break;
case 1:
a = p2; b = p1; fromIndex = i2; toIndex = i1; break;
case 2:
if (quad) { a = p1; b = p3; fromIndex = i1; toIndex = i3; }
else { a = p1; b = p0; fromIndex = i1; toIndex = i0; }
break;
default:
a = p3; b = p0; fromIndex = i3; toIndex = i0; break;
}

*(uint*)s_GteSxy806 = b;

for (short other = *neighbour; other >= 0; other = *++neighbour)
{

uint c1 = candidate[1];

|| edgeKey == ((c1 << 0x10) | (c0 >> 0x10))
|| (!cQuad && edgeKey == ((c0 << 0x10) | (c1 & 0xffff)))
|| (cQuad && (edgeKey == c1 || edgeKey == ((c0 << 0x10) | (c1 >> 0x10))));

if (cursor == target) written |= *(ushort*)((byte*)cursor + 2) & 0x800u;

*target = (written << 0x10) | (uint)(ushort)other;
else
*((byte*)target + 3) |= 8;

## ParseCmdLine
int nArgs = 1;
int nChars = 0;
byte* dst = argStorage;
byte* src = cmdLine;
void Emit(byte b)
{
nChars++;
if (dst != null) *dst++ = b;
}
if (argv != null) *argv++ = argStorage;
if (*src == (byte)'"')
{
byte* p = src + 1;
while (*p != (byte)'"' && *p != 0)
{
if (IsLeadByte(*p) && p[1] != 0)
{
Emit(*p); p++;
}
Emit(*p); p++;
}
Emit(0);
src = *p == (byte)'"' ? p + 1 : p;
}
else
{
while (*src > (byte)' ')
{
if (IsLeadByte(*src) && src[1] != 0)
{
Emit(*src); src++;
}
Emit(*src);
src++;
}
if (dst != null) dst--;
nChars--;
}
while (*src == ' ' || *src == '\t') src++;
bool inQuotes = false;
while (*src != 0)
{
if (argv != null) *argv++ = argStorage + nChars;
nArgs++;
while (true)
{
int backslashes = 0;
while (*src == '\\') { backslashes++; src++; }
if (*src == (byte)'"')
{
if ((backslashes & 1) == 0)
{
if (inQuotes && src[1] == (byte)'"') src++;
else inQuotes = !inQuotes;
}
backslashes /= 2;
}
for (; backslashes != 0; backslashes--) Emit((byte)'\\');
if (*src == 0 || (!inQuotes && (*src == ' ' || *src == '\t'))) break;
if (IsLeadByte(*src) && src[1] != 0)
{
Emit(*src); src++;
}
Emit(*src);
src++;
}
Emit(0);
while (*src == ' ' || *src == '\t') src++;
}
if (argv != null) *argv = null;
nArgs++;
argCount = nArgs;
charCount = nChars;

{

uint regionBase = (uint)(nint)mbi.BaseAddress;
uint regionSize = (uint)(nint)mbi.RegionSize;
if (regionSize == 0) break;
if (mbi.State == MEM_FREE)
{

{

if (highest > maxBase) highest = maxBase;
if (highest >= lowest)
{
found = true;
best = highest;
}
}
}
address = regionBase + regionSize;
}

}

## Synth_TextureLoadDib
int hr = -1;
Guarded("chargement de texture DIB", () =>
{
if (self == null || bitmapInfo == null || pixels == null)
throw new InvalidOperationException("argument nul");
var header = (uint*)bitmapInfo;
int width = (int)header[1];
int height = (int)header[2];
uint bitCount = header[3] >> 16;
bool bottomUp = height > 0;
if (!bottomUp) height = -height;
if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || width > 4096 || height > 4096)
throw new InvalidOperationException($"dimensions inattendues {width}x{height}");
if (bitCount != 4 && bitCount != 8)
throw new NotImplementedException(
$"DIB en {bitCount} bits par pixel — seuls 4 et 8 bits sont produits par " +
"FUN_0050fab0, le seul appelant connu de ce slot");
var palette = (uint*)((byte*)bitmapInfo + 0x28);
var source = new DibSource
{
Width = width,
Height = height,
BitCount = (int)bitCount,
BottomUp = bottomUp,
Indices = CopyDibIndices((byte*)pixels, width, height, (int)bitCount),
Palette = CopyPalette(palette),
};

D3D11Backend.RegisterTexture((nint)self, width, height, ExpandDib(source, -1, -1));
hr = 0;
if (s_DibLoads++ < 4)
Console.Error.WriteLine(
$"[SYNTH] texture DIB chargée 0x{(nint)self:X8} {width}x{height} " +
$"{bitCount} bits, {(bottomUp ? "bas-haut" : "haut-bas")}");
});
return hr;

{

int x0 = (short)v0;
int z0 = *(short*)(vertices + i0 * 8 + 4);

{
int x1 = (short)v1;

{

if ((attributes & 1) == 0)
{
third = (pz - z1) * (x0 - x1) - (px - x1) * (z0 - z1);
}
else
{

int z3 = v3[2];
int x3 = v3[0];
if ((pz - z1) * (x3 - x1) - (px - x1) * (z3 - z1) < 0) goto next;
third = (pz - z3) * (x0 - x3) - (px - x3) * (z0 - z3);

vec800[2] = z2 - z0;

vec799[2] = z1 - z0;

mac801[1] = vec800[2] * vec799[0] - vec799[2] * vec800[0];
mac801[2] = vec799[1] * vec800[0] - vec800[1] * vec799[0];

ir813[1] = (short)mac801[1];
ir813[2] = (short)mac801[2];

+ (pz - z0) * mac801[2]
